I just want people to actually look at the evidence I have.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed that the same people are also trying to harm, monitor, or interfere with you outside of work, or is it limited only to the workplace?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do believe that competing team has been monitoring my personal accounts outside of work to find ways to make me look bad. But even with them snooping, it hasn't stopped me from managing my life normally, like paying my bills on time or meeting up with my cousin.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any decline in your overall functioning outside of the work situation, such as trouble managing finances, self-care, or relationships because of these beliefs?\n\nPatient: No, my functioning outside of work is completely fine; I pay my bills on time, take care of myself, and meet my cousin for pho every weekend. The only real change is that I've pulled back from late-night basketball and social events because I have to stay on guard. Other than that, my day-to-day life is completely normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other unusual beliefs outside of this situation, such as feeling specially chosen, being controlled, or believing messages are hidden in ordinary events?\n\nPatient: No, I've never felt specially chosen, controlled, or that there are hidden messages in everyday things. My only concern is the very real, specific pattern of sabotage by that competing tech team at my work. There's nothing else going on besides me trying to protect my code.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other psychotic symptoms, such as hearing voices, seeing things others do not, or believing your thoughts are being controlled or broadcast to others?\n\nPatient: No, I don't hear voices, see things that aren't there, or feel like my thoughts are being controlled or broadcast at all. My mind is completely clear. My only concern is the very real, logical pattern of sabotage that I can see in my work logs.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered that there might be another explanation for what’s happening at work, or are you completely certain the competing team is targeting you even when others disagree?\n\nPatient: I am absolutely certain because the patterns in the logs and the timing of the glitches are far too precise to be coincidences. Even though IT and my manager claim they can't find anything, they simply aren't looking closely enough or don't understand the pattern like I do. There is no other logical explanation.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever been able to consider that this might be a misunderstanding or something other than deliberate targeting, even briefly, or are you completely convinced it is true despite what others say?\n\nPatient: No, it is definitely not a misunderstanding because the evidence in the logs and the timing of the glitches are just too clear to ignore. Even though IT and management claim they can't find anything, I am completely convinced that the other team is targeting me. There is simply no other way to explain what is happening.\n\nDoctor: Has this belief ever led you to take actions like confronting, reporting, or trying to retaliate against the people you think are targeting you?\n\nPatient: Yes, I did speak up during a sprint review meeting and accused a colleague from that team of spying on my work. It wasn't about retaliation, I was just pointing out the truth, but HR intervened and warned me that my position on the project is now at risk.\n\nDoctor: Have these beliefs about being targeted lasted continuously for at least 1 month, and have they stayed limited to this one topic without other psychotic symptoms?"

Subtypes: Diminished Emotional Expression, Avolition, Alogia, Anhedonia, Asociality\n[S024] Depressed_Mood: A state where a sad, empty, or hopeless mood persists. Subtypes: Significant Weight Loss, Weight Gain, Decrease in Appetite, Increase in Appetite\n[S027] Sleep_Disturbance: A state where there is a significant change in the quantity or quality of sleep.  Subtypes: Initial Insomnia, Middle Insomnia, Early Morning Awakening, Hypersomnia, Nightmare Disorder, Non-Restorative Sleep\n[S028] Psychomotor_Changes: Observable increase or decrease in psychomotor activity.  Subtypes: Psychomotor Agitation, Psychomotor Retardation (Motor), Psychomotor Retardation (Speech)\n[S029] Fatigue: Persistent fatigue that is not relieved by rest and a marked lack of energy.
